- [Search](https://help.tellius.com/getting-started/quick-start-guides/search.md): Search lets you ask natural-language questions, apply filters, compare periods, analyze trends, and drill into granular data—without SQL or dashboards.
- [Vizpads (Explore)](https://help.tellius.com/getting-started/quick-start-guides/vizpads-explore.md): Vizpads are interactive dashboards that let you explore data visually—add, resize, filter, format charts, and combine datasets for deeper insights.
- [Insights (Discover)](https://help.tellius.com/getting-started/quick-start-guides/insights-discover.md): Explore Tellius Insights to uncover key drivers, trends, cohorts, and anomalies—revealing patterns, root causes, and correlations hidden in your data.

## Tellius 6.3

- [Say Hello to Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius.md): New to Tellius? Explore our glossary, navigation tips, and guided tours to get started quickly and confidently.

- [Tellius 101](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/tellius-101.md): Tellius: AI analytics platform that connects data sources, with natural-language search, predictive models, and self-service BI to surface actionable insights.
- [Navigating around Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/navigating-around-tellius.md): Explore how to navigate Tellius—from Search and Vizpads to Insights, Predict, Data, and Projects—with a clear overview of every module and homepage feature.
- [Guided tours for quick onboarding](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/guided-tours-for-quick-onboarding.md): Quickly master Tellius modules with guided walkthroughs. Access interactive tutorials from the homepage to learn features, track progress, and view helpful videos.

- [Search](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/quick-start-guides/search.md): Search lets you ask natural-language questions, apply filters, compare periods, analyze trends, and drill into granular data—without SQL or dashboards.
- [Vizpads (Explore)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/quick-start-guides/vizpads-explore.md): Vizpads are interactive dashboards that let you explore data visually—add, resize, filter, format charts, and combine datasets for deeper insights.
- [Insights (Discover)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.3/getting-started/quick-start-guides/insights-discover.md): Explore Tellius Insights to uncover key drivers, trends, cohorts, and anomalies—revealing patterns, root causes, and correlations hidden in your data.

- [Search](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.2/getting-started/quick-start-guides/search.md): Search lets you ask natural-language questions, apply filters, compare periods, analyze trends, and drill into granular data—without SQL or dashboards.

- [Azure AD](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.2/settings/security/authentication/azure-ad.md): Configure Azure AD authentication to enable secure single sign-on in Tellius. Learn how to connect your tenant, set redirect URLs, and assign roles.
- [SAML](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.2/settings/security/authentication/saml.md): Learn how to configure SAML 2.0 authentication with your IdP (Okta, Azure AD, OneLogin) for secure Single Sign-On and automatic user provisioning.
- [Using Okta as an IdP (SAML Integration)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.2/settings/security/authentication/saml/using-okta-as-an-idp-saml-integration.md): Enable secure SSO for Tellius with Okta. Learn how to configure Okta as your SAML Identity Provider to streamline login and automatically provision users using corporate credentials.

- [OIDC](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.2/settings/security/authentication/oidc.md): Configure secure SSO with OIDC to enable seamless login through Okta, Google, or Azure AD. Set client details, redirect URL, and auto-provision user roles.

## Tellius 6.1

- [Say Hello to Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.1/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius.md): New to Tellius? Explore our glossary, navigation tips, and guided tours to get started quickly and confidently—whether you're a beginner or a returning user.
- [Glossary](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-6.1/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/glossary.md): Explore key Tellius terms with our easy-to-read glossary and learn how each feature helps you navigate the platform, analyze data, and gain insights.

## Tellius 5.5

- [Say Hello to Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius.md): New to Tellius? Explore our glossary, navigation tips, and guided tours to get started quickly and confidently. Whether you're a beginner or a returning user.

- [Tellius 101](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/tellius-101.md): Tellius 101 explains what Tellius is: an advanced analytics platform with AI insights, natural language search, Vizpads, and self-service BI to speed decisions.
- [Navigating around Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/navigating-around-tellius.md): Explore Tellius with ease: navigate the homepage, access Search, Explore, Discover, Predict, and manage Projects, Data, Insights, and more efficiently.
- [Guided tours for quick onboarding](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/say-hello-to-tellius/guided-tours-for-quick-onboarding.md): Use walkthroughs to get started with each module
- [Quick Start Guides](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/quick-start-guides.md): Jumpstart your Tellius journey with Quick Start Guides—learn Search, Vizpads, and Insights to master core analytics features.
- [Search](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/quick-start-guides/search.md): Learn how Tellius Search lets you ask natural-language questions, use keywords for filtering, grouping, comparisons, trends, and uncover insights without SQL or dashboards.
- [Vizpads (Explore)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/quick-start-guides/vizpads-explore.md): Explore Vizpads in Tellius—interactive, customizable dashboards that combine data, AI, Insights, and Search to help you build, analyze, and share dynamic visuals.
- [Insights (Discover)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/quick-start-guides/insights-discover.md): Explore Tellius security settings, including authentication options and configuration tools to keep your data protected and compliant.
- [Best Practices](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices.md): Explore Tellius Best Practices to maximize efficiency across Search, Vizpads, Insights, Data, and Predict modules with expert tips.
- [Search](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices/search.md): Learn how to get better results in Search by using synonyms, calculated columns, simple queries, the Search Inspector, and interactive exploration tools.
- [Vizpads (Explore)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices/vizpads-explore.md): Create interactive Vizpads in Tellius to visualize data, organize charts, apply filters, and manage dashboards efficiently for better analytics performance.
- [Insights (Discover)](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices/insights-discover.md): Discover how to use Insights to understand data trends, explore root causes, refine hypotheses, and optimize results by focusing only on relevant columns.
- [Predict](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices/predict.md): Get better model results by using the right data granularity, choosing suitable algorithms, handling nulls, and applying models to generate clear, actionable predictions.
- [Data](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/best-practices/data.md): Structure data effectively by defining granularity, refresh needs, relationships, naming rules, and Business View fields to ensure accurate analysis and strong performance.
- [Admin Guide](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/admin-guide.md): The Tellius Admin Guide covers system requirements, installation steps, and customization options to help you set up and optimize your environment.
- [Tellius architecture](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/admin-guide/tellius-architecture.md): Explore Tellius architecture—understand the web UI, natural language search, ML engines, and scalable storage powering AI-driven analytics.
- [System requirements](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/admin-guide/system-requirements.md): Review system requirements for deploying Tellius across on-prem, AWS, or Azure, including CPU, memory, storage, firewall ports, and access guidelines.

- [Customizing Tellius](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/getting-started/admin-guide/customizing-tellius.md): Customize Tellius by adding your logo and branding—set color palettes and UI styles to personalize the platform and create a consistent user experience.

- [Vizpads](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/vizpads.md): Configure Vizpad settings in Tellius to control data limits, batch rendering, and filter behavior for faster, smoother dashboards on large or live datasets.
- [Email](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/email.md): Configure SMTP in Tellius to enable alerts, notifications, and reports. Set authentication, encryption, and validation for reliable email delivery.
- [Anomalies](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/anomalies.md): Configure Anomalies settings in Tellius to control automatic anomaly detection, chart display limits, and visibility in Search or Vizpads for outlier analysis.
- [Query time](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/query-time.md): Get full visibility into your query performance. Learn to use the Query Time setting to proactively detect slow charts and boost loading speeds across your Vizpads.
- [Page 2](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/page-2.md)
- [Styles](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/styles.md): Customize the Tellius environment with Styles settings, upload logos, set browser titles, and define color palettes to match your organization’s brand.
- [Assistant](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/assistant.md): Configure the Assistant to customize its name, logo, and visibility. Enable natural language queries to help users explore data and discover insights.
- [Miscellaneous](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/miscellaneous.md): Configure Miscellaneous settings in Tellius to manage CDN usage, sharing permissions, and report rendering for better performance and secure collaboration.
- [Centralized Job Alerts](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/centralized-job-alerts.md): Learn how to configure centralized job alerts in Tellius to receive email notifications for job status, failures, partial successes, and scheduled executions.
- [Setting up LLM for Kaiya](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/settings/application-settings/setting-up-llm-for-kaiya.md): Set up LLMs for Kaiya to power natural language insights. Configure OpenAI, Gemini, or Azure OpenAI models with API keys, validation, and deployment options.

- [Patch 5.5.1](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.5/patch-5.5.1.md): Tellius 5.5.1 introduces dynamic metric match, smarter Kaiya SRL language understanding, and intelligent axis scaling—plus major UX and data fixes.
- [Patch 5.5.1.1](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.5/patch-5.5.1/patch-5.5.1.1.md): Tellius 5.5.1.1 introduces intent-based query learnings, enhanced SRL accuracy, stronger Vizpad exports, and dozens of fixes improving reliability and performance.
- [Patch 5.5.1.2](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.5/patch-5.5.1/patch-5.5.1.2.md): Tellius 5.5.1.2 improves Kaiya’s intent learnings, clarifications, and feedback, adds secure row-level filtering for embedded Vizpads, and fixes chart accuracy issues.
- [Patch 5.5.2](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.5/patch-5.5.2.md): Tellius 5.5.2 delivers critical fixes and performance improvements across Kaiya, Vizpads, Insights, and Data Management for a smoother, faster experience.
- [Release 5.4](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.4.md): Explore Tellius 5.4: Automate analytics with AI Agents, ask 'why' with Kaiya, utilize universal search, and customize insights with dynamic parameters.
- [Patches 5.4.0.1 to 5.4.0.4](https://help.tellius.com/tellius-documentation/whats-new/release-5.4/patches-5.4.0.1-to-5.4.0.4.md): Tellius patches 5.4.0.1–5.4.0.4 deliver stability, accuracy, and usability updates across Kaiya, Vizpads, Search, Insights, and Data Connectors.
